  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 112, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(112, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv_f): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(28, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
